# About this item

*    [Advanced Technology]: 2 to 37 foot pounds torque range with LED flashing/ buzzer alarm when torque is reached. Easy to read LCD display with measurements (kg-cm, N-m, in-lb. ft-lb) 
*    [Multiple Setting Functions]: Torque, Measurement, and, Peak & Trace, Mode Settings. (Torque setting range: 10% of max torque value. Measuring range: 2 to 37 ft-lbs.) 
*    [Quality Assurance]: Meets or exceeds ASME B107.14-2004, ISO 6789 standards, individually serialized with matching certificate of calibration traceable to N.I.S.T.

## Product Description

The ACDelco Brand has been around well over 100 years and its name has become synonymous with quality & reliability. ACDelco power tools are built for people who make a living with their tools, day in and day out, and require exceptional sturdiness and reliability. ACDelco offers the most complete line of digital torque wrench and adapter for professionals who requires high precision measurement on torque (ft-lbs./Nm) and angle degree for their job. Please use selection guides to choose a digital torque wrench with the right torque range for your application.

Customer Reviews, including Product Star Ratings help customers to learn more about the product and decide whether it is the right product for them.

To calculate the overall star rating and percentage breakdown by star, we don’t use a simple average. Instead, our system considers things like how recent a review is and if the reviewer bought the item on Amazon. It also analyzed reviews to verify trustworthiness.

### Customers say

Customers find the torque wrench to be a high-quality tool that works well, particularly for low to medium-low torque jobs, with accurate readings and good value for money. The warning sound receives mixed feedback - while customers appreciate the audible beeps that alert them to approaching set torque values, some mention the alarm starts beeping after exceeding the set value. The wrench's adjustability also gets mixed reviews, with customers noting non-intuitive controls. Durability is a concern as multiple customers report the product breaking during first use.

*   [![Image 100](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif) Vickey R](https://www.amazon.com/gp/profile/amzn1.account.AHY2YEC6K2ONB7AHKSJ5ZTB22CVA?ref=cm_cr_dp_d_bdcrb_top) _5 out of 5 stars_ ##### [Quality product. VERY useful, and not common, torque range](https://www.amazon.com/portal/customer-reviews/srp/-/RI5W9PIE5P7JB/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_ttl?_encoding=UTF8&ie=UTF8)Reviewed in the United States on November 19, 2024 [Style: Wrench](https://www.amazon.com/portal/customer-reviews/B004VYUQI2/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_fmt?_encoding=UTF8&formatType=current_format)[Verified Purchase](https://www.amazon.com/gp/help/customer/display.html/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_avp?ie=UTF8&nodeId=G8UYX7LALQC8V9KA)  Brief content visible, double tap to read full content. Full content visible, double tap to read brief content. It's easy to find wrenches with low max torque range or high max torque range. But "somewhere in between" was way more difficult than I thought? For me, the range of this wrench ACCURATELY provides meant I didn't have to buy TWO different wrenches ... nice!

I have a 20-150 ft-lb wrench for larger stuff, but for small-mid size fasteners (as typically found on cars and motorcycles) I wanted a wrench ACCURATE at the lower end of the torque spectrum which the 20-100+ ft-lb wrenches are NOT (read those calibration specs carefully, they all have caveats about their accuracy at the lower end of their range!)

Using the larger wrenches at the low end of their range (unless you have a high-end expensive model) is inviting disaster on smaller critical fasteners. So not worth it for me.

This wrench came with a calibration certification with my wrench's serial number on it and accuracy was more than acceptable for my use. It comes with a nice sturdy plastic case. Requires 4 AAA batteries which didn't come with it. Has a couple of neat features I may or may not use like "Trace"mode" which shows real-time torque as you tighten.

It's constructed, feels and handles like a top quality tool. How long it lasts and remains trouble free electronically remains to be seen ... but for now 5 stars. Full content visible, double tap to read brief content. Really enjoying this tool so far, 3 differnt units to toggle, a peak mode give you max torque it hit and a trace mode is nice for live reading, touch an existing tight bolt and trace will show the torque it's at as it starts to budge.

Flashing light looks a bit crappy, like a old school bulb LED and they were a bit tight not including batteries, I'd prefer add $2 to the cost and throw in the batteries for convenience, luckily I had some as I only opened the tool when I needed it.

I bought this for the mid range vales and I love the size of this over my 20" wrench.

Over all love it so far, using it to rebuild a Kubota engine.

I'll update if it doesn't keep working so well.       [Read more Read less](javascript:void(0))       One person found this helpful Helpful  Sending feedback...Thank you for your feedback. Full content visible, double tap to read brief content. UPDATE:

Figured the first issue was an anomaly, and I was in the middle of my Harley build, so I ordered another one - IT DID THE SAME EXACT THING!!! Luckily, I was within the return window still on this one and it is headed back! There seems to be an issue when you continuously use the clear function on the max torque feature. I would advise that you don’t use this feature. The manufacture must know about this defect as lightning, striking twice is not plausible, yet they continue to send these out with this seemingly software glitch. I will post a video.

Went slightly over the 30 day mark before first using those torque wrench on rebuilding my Harley. It worked perfectly at first for part of an afternoon and then when it shut down automatically it would not turn back on. I tried everything to correct the situation with new batteries, etc. but nothing works.

I contacted tech-support at AC Delco and I have to send the thing in on my dime to get it repaired. I’m very disappointed in this product. It was not worth the money. # Product Summary: ACDelco ARM601-3 3/8” (3.7 to 37 ft-lbs.) Digital Torque Wrench with Buzzer and LED Flash Notification – ISO 6789 Standards with Certificate of Calibration

From ACDelco

4.4 out of 5 stars, 1,871 ratings

[Customer reviews](https://www.amazon.com/dp/B004VYUQI2?ref=dp_product_quick_view#customer-reviews_feature_div)
50+ bought in past week

## Price

One-time purchase: $90.99

[Purchasing options](https://www.amazon.com/dp/B004VYUQI2?ref=dp_product_quick_view#buybox)

[Other sellers](https://www.amazon.com/dp/B004VYUQI2?ref=dp_product_quick_view#dynamic-aod-ingress-box)

## About this Item

*   [Advanced Technology]: 2 to 37 foot pounds torque range with LED flashing/ buzzer alarm when torque is reached. Easy to read LCD display with measurements (kg-cm, N-m, in-lb. ft-lb)
*   [Multiple Setting Functions]: Torque, Measurement, and, Peak & Trace, Mode Settings. (Torque setting range: 10% of max torque value. Measuring range: 2 to 37 ft-lbs.)
*   [Quality Assurance]: Meets or exceeds ASME B107.14-2004, ISO 6789 standards, individually serialized with matching certificate of calibration traceable to N.I.S.T.

## Product Description

 The ACDelco Brand has been around well over 100 years and its name has become synonymous with quality & reliability. ACDelco power tools are built for people who make a living with their tools, day in and day out, and require exceptional sturdiness and reliability. ACDelco offers the most complete line of digital torque wrench and adapter for professionals who requires high precision measurement on torque (ft-lbs./Nm) and angle degree for their job. Please use selection guides to choose a digital torque wrench with the right torque range for your application.
